 val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n            }\n            \n            // Update element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n elem_write = elems_out[i];\n    elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n    elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// One simulation step: every element is updated by an independent GPU thread\n__global__ void simulationStepKernel(const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elems_static,\n                                      const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elems_in,\n                                      ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elems_out,\n                                      const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n                                      size_t n_elems) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const ElementStatic& elem_static = elems_static[i];\n    const ElementDynamic elem_dyn = elems_in[i];\n    const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements\n    for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n        const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n        const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = elems_in[neighbor_idx];\n        total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    ElementDynamic& elem_write = elems_out[i];\n    elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n    elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    ElementStatic* d_static = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dynamic = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dynamic_swap = nullptr;\n    Material* d_materials = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_static, n_elems * sizeof(ElementStatic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dynamic, n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dynamic_swap, n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_materials, world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_static, world.elements_static.data(),\n                           n_elems * sizeof(ElementStatic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic.data(),\n                           n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_materials, world.materials.data(),\n                           world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    constexpr int block_size = 256;\n    const int grid_size = static_cast<int>((n_elems + block_size - 1) / block_size);\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        simulationStepKernel<<<grid_size, block_size>>>(d_static, d_dynamic, d_dynamic_swap,\n                                                          d_materials, n_elems);\n        std::swap(d_dynamic, d_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), d_dynamic,\n                           n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_static));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dynamic));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dynamic_swap));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_materials));\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n \n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"============================================\\n\");\n // Calculate memory usage\n    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;\n    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;\n    printf(\"Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\\n\",\n           total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start).count();\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration_ms);\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);\n    const double time_per_iter = static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;\n    const double giga_elems_per_sec = (n_measured_iters * n_elems) / (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    \n    // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS per element per iteration (from reference)\n    const double gflops = giga_elems_per_sec * 22.0;\n    \n %.4f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Compute hash for verification\n    const uint64_t hash = computeHash(world.elements_dynamic);\n    printf(\"  Result hash: %016lX\\n\", hash);\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        std::vector<double> energyData;\n        energyData.reserve(world.elements_dynamic.size());\n        for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n            energyData.push_back(elem.current_energy);\n        }\n        print_results(energyData, \"ElementEnergy\");\n    }\n    \n    // buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate without paying for a\n// serial host-side repacking pass.\n__global__ void transposeStaticKernel(const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elems_static,\n                                       idx_t* __restrict__ material_idx,\n                                       idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n                                       idx_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,\n                                       val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,\n                                       size_t n_elems) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n per-element connection lists) so that threads in the same warp\n// perform coalesced accesses instead of striding through large per-element\n// structs.\n__global__ void simulationStepKernel(const idx_t* __restrict__ material_idx,\n                                      const idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n                                      const idx_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,\n                                      const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,\n                                      const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elems_in,\n                                      ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elems_out,\n                                      const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n                                      size_t n_elems) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const idx_t nc = num_connections[i];\n    const Material& mat = materials[material_idx[i]];\n    const block_size>>>(d_static_aos, d_material_idx, d_num_connections,\n                                                       d_connected_idx, d_connected_flux, n_elems);\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), d_dynamic,\n                           n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n                                    \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Transpose the AoS connectivity data into SoA form (connection lists\n// transposed across elements) entirely on the GPU, in parallel, so that the\n// simulation kernel can perform coalesced accesses without paying for a\n// serial host-side repacking pass.\n__global__ void transposeStaticKernel(const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elems_static,\n                                       idx_t* __restrict__ material_idx,\n                                       idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n                                       idx_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,\n                                       val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,\n                                       size_t n_elems) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + connected_flux[conn_offset] = es.connected_flux[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// One simulation step: every element is updated by an independent GPU thread.\n// Connectivity is laid out as struct-of-arrays (transposed across the\n// fixed-size per-element connection lists) so that threads in the same warp\n// perform coalesced accesses instead of striding through large per-element\n// (neighbor_dyn.current_energy - elem_dyn.current_energy) *\n                      mat.transfer_coeff * flux_coeff * 0.25;\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    ElementDynamic& elem_write = elems_out[i];\n    elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n    elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    ElementStatic* d_static_aos = nullptr;\n    idx_t* d_material_idx =           world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    constexpr int block_size = 256;\n    const int grid_size = static_cast<int>((n_elems + block_size - 1) / std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Transpose the AoS connectivity data into SoA form (connection lists\n// transposed across elements) entirely on the GPU, in parallel, so that the\n// simulation kernel can perform coalesced accesses without paying for a\n// serial host-side repacking pass.\n__global__ void transposeStaticKernel(const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elems_static,\n                                       idx_t* __restrict__ material_idx,\n                                       idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n                                       idx_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,\n                                       val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,\n                                       size_t n_elems) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const ElementStatic& es = elems_static[i];\n    material_idx[i] = es.material_idx;\n    const idx_t nc = es.num_connections;\n    num_connections[i] = nc;\n    for (idx_t j = 0; j < nc; ++j) {\n        const size_t conn_offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;\n        connected_idx[conn_offset] = es.connected_idx[j];\n        connected_flux[conn_offset] = es.connected_flux[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// One simulation step: every element is updated by an independent GPU thread.\n// Connectivity is laid out as struct-of-arrays (transposed across the\n// fixed-size per-element connection lists) so that threads in the same warp\n// perform coalesced accesses instead of striding through large per-element\n// structs.\n__global__ void simulationStepKernel(const idx_t* __restrict__ material_idx,\n                                      const idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n                                      const idx_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,\n                                      const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,\n                                      const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elems_in,\n                                      ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elems_out,\n                                      const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n                                      size_t n_elems) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const idx_t nc = num_connections[i];\n    const Material& mat = materials[material_idx[i]];\n    const ElementDynamic elem_dyn = elems_in[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements\n    for (idx_t j = 0; j < nc; ++j) {\n        const size_t conn_offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;\n        const idx_t neighbor_idx = connected_idx[conn_offset];\n        const val_t flux_coeff = connected_flux[conn_offset];\n        const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = elems_in[neighbor_idx];\n        total_flux += (neighbor_dyn.current_energy - elem_dyn.current_energy) *\n                      mat.transfer_coeff * flux_coeff * 0.25;\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    ElementDynamic& elem_write = elems_out[i];\n    elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n    elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    ElementStatic* d_static_aos = nullptr;\n    idx_t* d_material_idx = nullptr;\n    idx_t* d_num_connections = nullptr;\n    idx_t* d_connected_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_connected_flux = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dynamic = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dynamic_swap = nullptr;\n    Material* d_materials = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_static_aos, n_elems * sizeof(ElementStatic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_material_idx, n_elems * sizeof(idx_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_num_connections, n_elems * sizeof(idx_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_connected_idx, static_cast<size_t>(MAX_CONNECTIONS) * n_elems * sizeof(idx_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_connected_flux, static_cast<size_t>(MAX_CONNECTIONS) * n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dynamic, n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dynamic_swap, n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_materials, world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_static_aos, world.elements_static.data(),\n                           n_elems * sizeof(ElementStatic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic.data(),\n                           n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_materials, world.materials.data(),\n                           world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    constexpr int block_size = 256;\n    const int grid_size = static_cast<int>((n_elems + block_size - 1) / block_size);\n\n    transposeStaticKernel<<<grid_size, block_size>>>(d_static_aos, d_material_idx, d_num_connections,\n                                                       d_connected_idx, d_connected_flux, n_elems);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_static_aos));\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        simulationStepKernel<<<grid_size, block_size>>>(\n            d_material_idx, d_num_connections, d_connected_idx, d_connected_flux,\n            d_dynamic, d_dynamic_swap, d_materials, n_elems);\n        std::swap(d_dynamic, d_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), d_dynamic,\n                           n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_material_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_num_connections));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_connected_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_connected_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dynamic));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dynamic_swap));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_materials));\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"============================================\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %d x %d =   \n    // Build the unstructured mesh\n    // Force lazy CUDA context initialization here so it isn't attributed to\n    // the timed simulation below.\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0));\n\n    printf(\"Building unstructured mesh...\\n\");\n    World world;\n    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root);\n    \n    // Calculate memory usage\n    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;\n    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;\n    printf(\"Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\\n\",\n           total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration_ms);\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);\n    const double time_per_iter = {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CeeXYx836k2qDD5XWWiKh","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The CUDA parallelization is complete and verified. Summary of changes:\n\n**`unstructured/unstructured.cpp`**\n- Added `<cuda_runtime.h>` and a `CUDA_CHECK` error-checking macro.\n- Replaced the CPU `runSimulation` loop with a GPU implementation:\n  - `transposeStaticKernel`: parallel GPU kernel that converts the connectivity data from array-of-structs to struct-of-arrays (transposed connection lists) for coalesced memory access — done once, on-device, avoiding a slow serial host-side repack.\n  - `simulationStepKernel`: one CUDA thread per mesh element, computing flux from all connections and writing to a ping-pong output buffer — bit-for-bit equivalent arithmetic/order to the original `computeFlux`.\n  - Host-device buffer swapping is done via pointer swaps between iterations (no unnecessary host round-trips); result is copied back to host only after all iterations complete.\n  - Added a `cudaFree(0)` warm-up before the timed region so one-time CUDA context initialization isn't counted as simulation time (this doesn't change program semantics/output, only what's included in the reported timing, matching the original benchmark's intent).\n\n**`unstructured/CMakeLists.txt`**\n- Enabled the `CUDA` language, set `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES` (before `project()`, since setting it after has no effect) to target the RTX 3090 (`sm_86`), and compiled `unstructured.cpp` as CUDA source via `set_source_files_properties(... LANGUAGE CUDA)` — this keeps the single-file, single-executable structure with no new files.\n- Added `-O3 --use_fast_math` CUDA compile flags and enabled CUDA separable compilation.\n\n**Verification**: Built a temporary CPU-only reference from the original algorithm and confirmed the `computeHash` output matches exactly across multiple grid sizes and iteration counts (512×512/10 iters, 256×256/20 iters, 1024×1024/5 iters), confirming bit-identical numerical results.
